// ------------------------------------------------------------
fdLvEst(leSt, leSx)
BEGIN
  LOCAL leI, leS, leM, leD, leK, leSm;
  LOCAL leV, leSd, leH, leHx, leNo, leN;
  leK := 0; leSm := 0; leV := 0; leSd := 0;
  leH := 0; leHx := 0; leNo := 0; leN := 0;
  FOR leI FROM 1 TO 7 DO
    leS := leSt(leI);
    IF leS > 0 THEN
      leN := leN + 1;
      IF leS == 8 THEN
        // stage H: a floor, not an age
        leH := leH + 1;
        leHx := MAX(leHx, fdLvH(leSx, leI));
      ELSE
        leM := fdLvMn(leSx, leI, leS);
        IF leM > 0 THEN
          leD := fdLvSD(leSx, leI, leS);
          leK := leK + 1;
          leSm := leSm + leM;
          leV := leV + leD^2;
          leSd := leSd + leD;
        ELSE
          leNo := leNo + 1;
        END;
      END;
    END;
  END;
  IF leK == 0 THEN RETURN {0, 0, 0, 0, 0, leH, leHx, leNo, leN}; END;
  leM := leSm / leK;
  leD := leSd / leK;
  RETURN {leK, leM, leV^0.5 / leK, leD, NORMALD_CDF(leM, leD, 2*leM - 18),
          leH, leHx, leNo, leN};
END;

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// The WILLEMS result page (v3.8): "WILLEMS (2001): the published
// method".  Shows the seven scores and their sum, the paper's error,
// the 68 % and 95 % ranges and the verdict band -- or, when there is
// no Willems age, exactly why (which tooth is not staged, which is at
// a stage Willems printed no score for) and no number at all.  (v3.9:
// at the tables' ceiling, fdWCeil, the sum is shown as "at least about
// 15.8-16", with no ranges and no band.)
//   wpSt  the seven stage columns;  wpSx  the Setup sex (0, 1, 2)
// Returns fdWEst's list, for the pool question.
// ------------------------------------------------------------
fdWPage(wpSt, wpSx)
BEGIN
  LOCAL wpR, wpL, wpI, wpS, wpV, wpT;
  LOCAL wpLn, wpU, wpW, wpX;
  // (STRING(30 + wpI, 1, 0) below is tooth wpI's FDI number, 31..37;
  // MID("ABCDEFGH", stage, 1) its stage letter)
  wpR := fdWEst(wpSt, wpSx);
  IF wpR(1) == 1 THEN
    IF wpSx == 0 THEN
      // the sex unknown: the stages, both sums, their mean
      wpLn := "Stages:";
      FOR wpI FROM 1 TO 7 DO
        wpLn := wpLn + " " + STRING(30 + wpI, 1, 0) + MID("ABCDEFGH", wpSt(wpI), 1);
      END;
      wpL := {"Sex unknown: both tables, 50:50.", wpLn,
              "Boys  (Table 1): sum " + fdF2(wpR(5)) + " yr",
              "Girls (Table 2): sum " + fdF2(wpR(6)) + " yr"};
    ELSE
      // one sex: each tooth's score, three to a line, then the sum
      wpW := "Boys' scores (Table 1), summed:";
      IF wpSx == 2 THEN wpW := "Girls' scores (Table 2), summed:"; END;
      wpL := {wpW};
      wpLn := "";
      FOR wpI FROM 1 TO 7 DO
        wpS := wpSt(wpI);
        wpT := STRING(30 + wpI, 1, 0) + " " + MID("ABCDEFGH", wpS, 1) + " " + fdF2(fdWSc(wpSx, wpI, wpS));
        IF DIM(wpLn) > 0 THEN wpLn := wpLn + "   "; END;
        wpLn := wpLn + wpT;
        IF wpI == 3 OR wpI == 6 OR wpI == 7 THEN
          wpL := CONCAT(wpL, {wpLn});
          wpLn := "";
        END;
      END;
    END;
    IF wpR(11) == 1 THEN
      // v3.9 (fleet review C-F1; second review): AT THE TABLES' CEILING
      // (fdWCeil: teeth 33-37 all at H).  The largest sums any staging
      // gives are 16.03 (boys) and 15.93 (girls), and every dentition
      // past about 16 is there.  Willems's children were at most 18.0,
      // so the sum says only "at least about 15.8-16": no 68 % / 95 %
      // lines, no verdict band (vp = -1), and the pool is not offered
      // this age (FDAES(), Roger's D7).  Before v3.9 this page printed
      // "Dental age 16.03 yr" and a red band, "likely < 18  P(age >= 18)
      // = 1.4%", for a dentition every adult has.  wpX: the first line,
      // all seven at H or only 33-37.
      wpX := "= 33-37 at H: the tables' ceiling";
      IF wpSt(1) == 8 AND wpSt(2) == 8 THEN wpX := "= All seven at H: the tables' ceiling"; END;
      IF wpSx == 0 THEN
        wpL := CONCAT(wpL, {wpX,
                            "The largest sums any staging gives:",
                            "16.03 boys, 15.93 girls. Willems's",
                            "children were at most 18.0, so sums",
                            "this near the top cannot tell 16",
                            "from 30: they say only 'at least",
                            "about 15.8-16'.",
                            ("No P(age " + CHAR(8805) + " 18), nothing to pool."),
                            "One score alone is not an age."});
      ELSE
        wpL := CONCAT(wpL, {wpX,
                            "Sum " + fdF2(wpR(2)) + " yr; the largest any",
                            "staging gives: 16.03 boys, 15.93",
                            "girls. Willems's children were at",
                            "most 18.0, so a sum this near the",
                            "top cannot tell 16 from 30: it says",
                            "only 'at least about 15.8-16'.",
                            ("No P(age " + CHAR(8805) + " 18), nothing to pool.")});
      END;
      // no number on this page: the Liversidge page follows, so "next"
      fdFtX := "any key or tap = next";
      fdPageV("WILLEMS (2001): the published method", wpL, -1);
    ELSE
      IF wpSx == 0 THEN
        wpL := CONCAT(wpL, {"= Dental age " + fdF2(wpR(2)) + " yr (their mean)",
                            "Test (p 894): s.d. 0.9 boys, 1.3",
                            "girls; mean diff. (signed) 0.0, +0.2",
                            "50:50 mixture: SD " + fdF2(wpR(3)) + " yr"});
      ELSE
        wpL := CONCAT(wpL, {"= Dental age " + fdF2(wpR(2)) + " yr"});
        IF wpSx == 1 THEN
          wpL := CONCAT(wpL, {"Paper's test (p 894), boys:", "mean diff. (signed) +0.0, s.d. 0.9 yr"});
        ELSE
          wpL := CONCAT(wpL, {"Paper's test (p 894), girls:", "mean diff. (signed) +0.2, s.d. 1.3 yr"});
        END;
      END;
      // the ranges: +/- 1 and 2 s.d. (the mixture's SD with the sex
      // unknown), as every other result page in FDAES gives them
      wpL := CONCAT(wpL, {"68%: [" + fdF1(MAX(0, wpR(2) - wpR(3))) + ", " + fdF1(wpR(2) + wpR(3)) + "] yr",
                          "95%: [" + fdF1(MAX(0, wpR(2) - 2*wpR(3))) + ", " + fdF1(wpR(2) + 2*wpR(3)) + "] yr",
                          "(+ = too old; not subtracted here)"});
      IF wpSx == 0 THEN
        wpL := CONCAT(wpL, {"One score alone is not an age."});
      ELSE
        wpL := CONCAT(wpL, {"One score alone is not an age:", "only the sum of all seven is."});
      END;
      fdPageV("WILLEMS (2001): the published method", wpL, wpR(4));
    END;
  ELSE
    // no Willems age: say exactly why, and show no number
    wpL := {"No age: Willems (2001) adds a score",
            "for each of the seven left lower",
            "teeth, and gives no rule for a",
            "tooth that is missing or unscored.",
            ""};
    // the teeth not staged
    wpU := 0;
    wpLn := "";
    FOR wpI FROM 1 TO 7 DO
      IF wpSt(wpI) == 0 THEN
        wpU := wpU + 1;
        IF DIM(wpLn) > 0 THEN wpLn := wpLn + ", "; END;
        wpLn := wpLn + STRING(30 + wpI, 1, 0);
      END;
    END;
    IF wpU == 1 THEN wpL := CONCAT(wpL, {"Needs all seven teeth: " + wpLn + " not staged"}); END;
    IF wpU > 1 THEN wpL := CONCAT(wpL, {"Needs all seven teeth; not staged:", wpLn}); END;
    // the staged teeth at a stage the table in use did not score:
    // wpV adds 1 when the boys' table has no score, 2 when the girls'
    FOR wpI FROM 1 TO 7 DO
      wpS := wpSt(wpI);
      IF wpS > 0 THEN
        wpV := 0;
        IF fdWSc(1, wpI, wpS) < -50 THEN wpV := wpV + 1; END;
        IF fdWSc(2, wpI, wpS) < -50 THEN wpV := wpV + 2; END;
        wpT := STRING(30 + wpI, 1, 0) + " at " + MID("ABCDEFGH", wpS, 1) + ": ";
        wpX := "";
        IF wpSx == 1 AND (wpV == 1 OR wpV == 3) THEN wpX := wpT + "Willems printed no score"; END;
        IF wpSx == 2 AND wpV >= 2 THEN wpX := wpT + "Willems printed no score"; END;
        IF wpSx == 0 AND wpV == 1 THEN wpX := wpT + "no score for boys"; END;
        IF wpSx == 0 AND wpV == 2 THEN wpX := wpT + "no score for girls"; END;
        IF wpSx == 0 AND wpV == 3 THEN wpX := wpT + "no score for either sex"; END;
        IF DIM(wpX) > 0 THEN wpL := CONCAT(wpL, {wpX}); END;
      END;
    END;
    wpL := CONCAT(wpL, {"", "Liversidge (next page) still reads", "each staged tooth."});
    // v3.8a: the Liversidge page comes next, so the footer says "next"
    fdFtX := "any key or tap = next";
    fdPageV("WILLEMS (2001): the published method", wpL, -1);
  END;
  RETURN wpR;
END;

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// The LIVERSIDGE result page (v3.8): "LIVERSIDGE (2006): tooth by
// tooth".  Each staged tooth with its in-stage mean and SD (Table
// X), or "at least" at H (Table IX), or "no reference" at an early
// stage; then the combined estimate with BOTH SEs, why the larger
// one is nearer the truth, and the verdict band from N(estimate,
// the larger SE) -- v3.9: no band when a tooth at H puts a floor
// above the estimate.  With every staged tooth at H there is no
// estimate: only "at least" the largest floor.
//   lpSt  the seven stage columns;  lpSx  the Setup sex (0, 1, 2)
// Returns fdLvEst's list, for the pool question.
// ------------------------------------------------------------
fdLPage(lpSt, lpSx)
BEGIN
  LOCAL lpR, lpL, lpI, lpS, lpM, lpT;
  LOCAL lpW, lpB;
  lpR := fdLvEst(lpSt, lpSx);
  lpW := "girls and boys";
  IF lpSx == 1 THEN lpW := "boys"; END;
  IF lpSx == 2 THEN lpW := "girls"; END;
  lpL := {"Mean age of the children IN each",
          "stage, Table X, " + lpW + ":"};
  // one line per staged tooth, 31 to 37
  FOR lpI FROM 1 TO 7 DO
    lpS := lpSt(lpI);
    IF lpS > 0 THEN
      lpT := STRING(30 + lpI, 1, 0) + " " + MID("ABCDEFGH", lpS, 1) + "  ";
      IF lpS == 8 THEN
        lpT := lpT + "at least " + fdF2(fdLvH(lpSx, lpI)) + " yr (Table IX)";
      ELSE
        lpM := fdLvMn(lpSx, lpI, lpS);
        IF lpM > 0 THEN
          lpT := lpT + fdF2(lpM) + " yr  SD " + fdF2(fdLvSD(lpSx, lpI, lpS));
        ELSE
          lpT := lpT + "no reference (too early)";
        END;
      END;
      lpL := CONCAT(lpL, {lpT});
    END;
  END;
  IF lpR(1) > 0 THEN
    // v3.8a: "Mean of 1 tooth", "Mean of 3 teeth"
    lpL := CONCAT(lpL, {"= Mean of " + STRING(lpR(1), 1, 0) + IFTE(lpR(1) == 1, " tooth: ", " teeth: ") + fdF2(lpR(2)) + " yr",
                        "SE if independent: " + fdF2(lpR(3)) + " yr",
                        "SE if they move together: " + fdF2(lpR(4)) + " yr",
                        "Teeth in one child develop together,",
                        "so the truth is nearer the larger SE.",
                        ("P(age " + CHAR(8805) + " 18) from N(") + fdF2(lpR(2)) + ", " + fdF2(lpR(4)) + ")."});
    // v3.9 (fleet review C-F6): the verdict band's P comes from
    // N(estimate, the larger SE).  When a tooth at H puts a floor ABOVE
    // the estimate, that normal model contradicts the floor (it could
    // read "probably < 18" under a floor of 15.25), so no band is drawn.
    lpB := lpR(5);
    IF lpR(6) > 0 THEN
      lpL := CONCAT(lpL, {"At H: at least " + fdF2(lpR(7)) + " yr, the mean",
                          "age of reaching H (some reach it",
                          "earlier)."});
      IF lpR(7) > lpR(2) THEN
        lpL := CONCAT(lpL, {"That floor is above the estimate,",
                            ("so no P(age " + CHAR(8805) + " 18) band is drawn.")});
        lpB := -1;
      END;
    END;
    fdPageV("LIVERSIDGE (2006): tooth by tooth", lpL, lpB);
  ELSE
    IF lpR(6) > 0 THEN
      lpL := CONCAT(lpL, {"No estimate: Table X leaves out H,",
                          "and no tooth below H has a figure.",
                          "= At least " + fdF2(lpR(7)) + " yr (Table IX: the",
                          "mean age of reaching H)."});
    ELSE
      lpL := CONCAT(lpL, {"No estimate: no staged tooth has a",
                          "Table X figure (its stage is too",
                          "early for children aged 2 to 17)."});
    END;
    fdPageV("LIVERSIDGE (2006): tooth by tooth", lpL, -1);
  END;
  RETURN lpR;
END;

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// TWO WAYS TO READ THE SAME TEETH (v3.8): the teaching page, from
// the Willems module's pool question and from About.
// ------------------------------------------------------------
fdTwoWays()
BEGIN
  fdPage("TWO WAYS TO READ THE SAME TEETH", {
    "WILLEMS (2001) adds seven scores.",
    "They were fitted together, so one",
    "alone means nothing; some are even",
    "negative (girls' P1 at A: -0.95).",
    "Only the sum of all seven is an age.",
    "",
    "LIVERSIDGE (2006) gives each tooth",
    "its own ages: the mean and SD of the",
    "children found in that stage.",
    "",
    "Averaging k teeth: if they were",
    "independent the SD would shrink by",
    (CHAR(8730) + "(k). They are not: one child's"),
    "teeth develop together, so the",
    "truth is nearer (sum of SD) / k,",
    "the larger SE.",
    "",
    "An 'in stage' mean also depends on",
    "the ages of the children sampled.",
    "Liversidge's were 2 to 17: nobody",
    "older, so stage H has no mean at",
    "all, only a floor (the mean age of",
    "reaching H).",
    "",
    "Pool one or the other, not both:",
    "they read the same seven teeth."});
END;

// Stage colour, light to dark: A is the house light blue
// RGB(235,242,255), H the house blue RGB(0,60,200), the six
// between evenly spaced.
fdStCol(stg)
BEGIN
  LOCAL scT;
  scT := (stg - 1) / 7;
  RETURN RGB(IP(235 - 235*scT), IP(242 - 182*scT), IP(255 - 55*scT));
END;

// A tile with rounded corners: a filled RECT_P inset by one pixel,
// four edges stopping two pixels short of the corners, and one
// pixel set diagonally in each corner.
fdTile(tx1, ty1, tx2, ty2, tfill, tedge)
BEGIN
  RECT_P(G1, tx1 + 1, ty1 + 1, tx2 - 1, ty2 - 1, tfill, tfill);
  LINE_P(G1, tx1 + 2, ty1, tx2 - 2, ty1, tedge);
  LINE_P(G1, tx1 + 2, ty2, tx2 - 2, ty2, tedge);
  LINE_P(G1, tx1, ty1 + 2, tx1, ty2 - 2, tedge);
  LINE_P(G1, tx2, ty1 + 2, tx2, ty2 - 2, tedge);
  PIXON_P(G1, tx1 + 1, ty1 + 1, tedge);
  PIXON_P(G1, tx2 - 1, ty1 + 1, tedge);
  PIXON_P(G1, tx1 + 1, ty2 - 1, tedge);
  PIXON_P(G1, tx2 - 1, ty2 - 1, tedge);
END;

// A red "you are here" ring two pixels wide, three pixels outside
// a tile (outline only: RECT_P would fill it).
fdRing(rx1, ry1, rx2, ry2)
BEGIN
  LOCAL rgI;
  FOR rgI FROM 2 TO 3 DO
    LINE_P(G1, rx1 - rgI, ry1 - rgI, rx2 + rgI, ry1 - rgI, #FF0000h);
    LINE_P(G1, rx1 - rgI, ry2 + rgI, rx2 + rgI, ry2 + rgI, #FF0000h);
    LINE_P(G1, rx1 - rgI, ry1 - rgI, rx1 - rgI, ry2 + rgI, #FF0000h);
    LINE_P(G1, rx2 + rgI, ry1 - rgI, rx2 + rgI, ry2 + rgI, #FF0000h);
  END;
END;

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// The little tooth on each stage button (v3.6; redrawn in v3.7b).
// It draws the stages of Demirjian, Goldstein & Tanner (1973), the
// stages Willems (2001) and Mincer et al. (1993) use (the molar
// definitions are in the header, STAGE WORDING):
//   A  cusp tips calcified, not     two separate cusp tips
//      fused
//   B  the tips fused, occlusal     the tips joined (2 rows)
//      outline regular
//   C  occlusal enamel complete,    the occlusal half of the crown
//      dentine begun                (6 rows of 12)
//   D  crown complete to the CEJ,   the whole crown (12 rows) and
//      root formation begun         a root just begun (3 rows)
//   E  root shorter than the crown  root 8 rows, crown 12
//   F  root at least as long as     root 14 rows; its last 3 rows
//      the crown, apex funnel-      flare outward (wide open)
//      shaped
//   G  canal walls parallel, apex   the full root (18 rows), walls
//      still partly open            parallel, no closing line
//   H  apex closed                  the full root, closed by a line
// pcCr and pcRt hold the rows drawn (crown of 12, root of 18; the
// crown's 12 rows are the yardstick for E and F, so E's root must
// stay below 12 rows and F's at 12 or more).  F's flare and H's
// closing line are the two special cases below.  The full tooth is
// outlined faintly first, so the formed part reads as a FRACTION
// of it.  (v3.6 drew this program's own wording: C a third of the
// crown, D half of it, E the whole crown with no root, F a third
// of the root, G two thirds.)
//   pcX, pcY top-left of a 12 x 30 box; pcS stage 1..8; pcC colour
// ------------------------------------------------------------
fdPicto(pcX, pcY, pcS, pcC)
BEGIN
  LOCAL pcCr, pcRt, pcG, pcEnd;
  pcCr := {2, 2, 6, 12, 12, 12, 12, 12};   // crown rows formed, of 12
  pcRt := {0, 0, 0, 3, 8, 14, 18, 18};     // root rows formed, of 18
  pcG := #AAB4C8h;                         // the faint full outline
  LINE_P(G1, pcX, pcY, pcX + 11, pcY, pcG);
  LINE_P(G1, pcX, pcY, pcX, pcY + 11, pcG);
  LINE_P(G1, pcX + 11, pcY, pcX + 11, pcY + 11, pcG);
  LINE_P(G1, pcX + 2, pcY + 12, pcX + 2, pcY + 29, pcG);
  LINE_P(G1, pcX + 9, pcY + 12, pcX + 9, pcY + 29, pcG);
  // the crown: A two separate tips, otherwise the rows formed
  IF pcS == 1 THEN
    RECT_P(G1, pcX, pcY, pcX + 3, pcY + 1, pcC, pcC);
    RECT_P(G1, pcX + 8, pcY, pcX + 11, pcY + 1, pcC, pcC);
  ELSE
    RECT_P(G1, pcX, pcY, pcX + 11, pcY + pcCr(pcS) - 1, pcC, pcC);
  END;
  // the root walls, from the CEJ (row 12) down to row pcEnd
  IF pcRt(pcS) > 0 THEN
    pcEnd := pcY + 11 + pcRt(pcS);
    IF pcS == 6 THEN
      // F: straight walls, then a funnel -- the last three rows
      // flare outward, so the apex is wide open
      LINE_P(G1, pcX + 2, pcY + 12, pcX + 2, pcEnd - 3, pcC);
      LINE_P(G1, pcX + 9, pcY + 12, pcX + 9, pcEnd - 3, pcC);
      LINE_P(G1, pcX + 2, pcEnd - 3, pcX, pcEnd, pcC);
      LINE_P(G1, pcX + 9, pcEnd - 3, pcX + 11, pcEnd, pcC);
    ELSE
      LINE_P(G1, pcX + 2, pcY + 12, pcX + 2, pcEnd, pcC);
      LINE_P(G1, pcX + 9, pcY + 12, pcX + 9, pcEnd, pcC);
    END;
  END;
  // H: the apex closed (G, with the same full walls, stays open)
  IF pcS == 8 THEN LINE_P(G1, pcX + 2, pcY + 29, pcX + 9, pcY + 29, pcC); END;
END;

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// THE ODONTOGRAM (v3.6; seven teeth and Liversidge's ages from v3.8)
// -- the staging screen of the Willems / Liversidge module.
// The mandibular left quadrant, midline at the left: the seven teeth
// 31 .. 37 as tiles along a gentle arch.
//   Tap a tooth: it gets a red ring and the stage buttons A-H
//     appear below it.  Tap a stage: the tooth fills with that
//     stage's colour (light A to dark H).  Tap the same stage again
//     to un-stage the tooth.
//   Left / Right choose a tooth, Up / Down step its stage (below A
//     is "not staged"), Enter opens the stage list as a menu (the
//     keyboard route), + = Done, Esc = Cancel.
//   Soft keys: Clear (un-stage all), Cancel, Done.
// A staged tile shows its stage letter and, for the Setup sex (boys,
// girls, or girls and boys when the sex is unknown), Liversidge's
// (2006) age for that tooth and stage:
//   stages A-G  Table X: the mean age of the children IN that stage,
//               e.g. "35 F 11.0" (boys);
//   stage H     ">=" and Table IX's mean age of ATTAINING H, to two
//               decimals as printed (Table X has no H; with the sex
//               unknown, the girls' age);
//   no figure   a dash: an early stage, before the sample's ages.
// The panel, top to bottom:
//   1  the selected tooth and its stage label (Demirjian 1973);
//   2  Liversidge's figure for that tooth, in words;
//   3  Liversidge for the staged teeth below H: the mean of their
//      in-stage means and its two SEs, independent and together;
//   4  the teeth at H: "at least" the largest of their floors;
//   5  Willems (2001): "n of 7 staged" until all seven have a printed
//      score, then the sum (magenta, a derived quantity); v3.9: at the
//      tables' ceiling (fdWCeil: 33-37 at H) it reads "at the ceiling,
//      >= ~15.8-16 yr" and its mark on the axis points older;
// then the age axis, 2 to 20 years with a red dotted line at 18:
// each staged tooth's in-stage mean as a pale blue tick, Liversidge's
// estimate as a blue square with a bar of +/- the larger SE, the H
// floor as a blue arrow pointing older, Willems's sum as a magenta
// mark.  The panel and the result pages call the SAME routines
// (fdLvEst, fdWEst), so they cannot disagree.
// Returns the seven stage columns (0 = not staged, 1..8 = A..H) on
// Done, or {-1} on Cancel.  wtn holds the seven tooth names.
// ------------------------------------------------------------
fdOdonto(wtn, sx)
BEGIN
  LOCAL odSt, odSel, odGo, odRet, odK, odM;
  LOCAL odM1, odX, odY, odI, odS, odV;
  LOCAL odX1, odX2, odY1, odY2, odFdi, odStg;
  LOCAL odPx, odTx, odCol, odCh, odAct, odCx;
  LOCAL odL, odW, odB, odWr, odLr;

  // tile geometry for 31 .. 37 (index 1 .. 7): one list per edge,
  // shared by the drawing and the tap test.  The incisors are the
  // narrowest tiles (28 and 30 px), the molars the widest (50 and
  // 48 px), 4 px apart; the tops follow a gentle arch.  Each tile is
  // 52 px tall, four rows: the FDI number (top), the stage letter,
  // ">=" (stage H only) and Liversidge's age (bottom).
  odX1 := {24, 55, 88, 125, 162, 199, 252};
  odX2 := {51, 84, 121, 158, 195, 248, 299};
  odY1 := {38, 35, 32, 30, 28, 27, 27};
  odY2 := {89, 86, 83, 81, 79, 78, 78};
  odFdi := {"31", "32", "33", "34", "35", "36", "37"};
  // the stage labels (v3.7b): Demirjian, Goldstein & Tanner (1973),
  // condensed; the same strings as the Enter menu below and the M3
  // grade menu.  At most 27 characters, so the panel line (tooth
  // name, ": ", label) fits its 305 px.
  odStg := {"A  cusp tips, not joined", "B  cusp tips joined",
            "C  occlusal enamel complete", "D  crown complete to CEJ",
            "E  root shorter than crown", ("F  root " + CHAR(8805) + " crown, apex wide"),
            "G  canal walls parallel", "H  apex closed"};
  // the Setup sex as a word for the panel: Table X's three columns
  odW := "children";
  IF sx == 1 THEN odW := "boys"; END;
  IF sx == 2 THEN odW := "girls"; END;
  odSt := {0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0};
  odSel := 0; odGo := 1; odRet := {-1};
  REPEAT odK := GETKEY; UNTIL odK == -1;
  fdDrain();

  WHILE odGo DO
    // ---- the two estimates for the teeth staged so far, from the
    // SAME routines as the result pages
    odWr := fdWEst(odSt, sx);
    odLr := fdLvEst(odSt, sx);

    // ---------------- compose the frame in G1 ----------------
    DIMGROB_P(G1, 320, 240, #FFFFFFh);
    RECT_P(G1, 0, 0, 319, 22, #FF4500h, #FF4500h);
    TEXTOUT_P("MANDIBLE LEFT  Willems + Liversidge", G1, 6, 4, 2, #FFFFFFh, 308);
    // the midline
    fdDotV(18, 35, 92, #5A5A5Ah);
    TEXTOUT_P("mid", G1, 4, 24, 1, #5A5A5Ah);

    // the seven teeth
    FOR odI FROM 1 TO 7 DO
      odS := odSt(odI);
      odCx := IP((odX1(odI) + odX2(odI)) / 2);
      IF odS > 0 THEN
        odCol := fdStCol(odS);
        IF odS >= 5 THEN odTx := #FFFFFFh; ELSE odTx := #000000h; END;
        fdTile(odX1(odI), odY1(odI), odX2(odI), odY2(odI), odCol, #003CC8h);
        TEXTOUT_P(MID("ABCDEFGH", odS, 1), G1, odCx - 4, odY1(odI) + 14, 2, odTx);
        // Liversidge's age for this tooth at this stage, to one
        // decimal ("-" where Table X has none); at H the floor from
        // Table IX, marked ">=", with its two decimals as printed so a
        // lower bound is never rounded UP (9.97 must not read 10.0)
        IF odS == 8 THEN
          TEXTOUT_P(CHAR(8805), G1, odCx - 6, odY1(odI) + 27, 1, odTx);
          odL := fdF2(fdLvH(sx, odI));
        ELSE
          odV := fdLvMn(sx, odI, odS);
          odL := "-";
          IF odV > 0 THEN odL := fdF1(odV); END;
        END;
        TEXTOUT_P(odL, G1, odCx - 3*DIM(odL), odY1(odI) + 38, 1, odTx);
      ELSE
        fdTile(odX1(odI), odY1(odI), odX2(odI), odY2(odI), #FFFFFFh, #5A5A5Ah);
        odTx := #5A5A5Ah;
      END;
      TEXTOUT_P(odFdi(odI), G1, odCx - 6, odY1(odI) + 3, 1, odTx);
    END;
    // the red ring round the selected tooth, drawn after all seven
    // tiles so that the next tile cannot cover part of it
    IF odSel > 0 THEN
      fdRing(odX1(odSel), odY1(odSel), odX2(odSel), odY2(odSel));
    END;

    // the stage buttons, while a tooth is selected; else the help
    IF odSel > 0 THEN
      FOR odS FROM 1 TO 8 DO
        odPx := 6 + (odS - 1)*39;
        odCol := fdStCol(odS);
        IF odS >= 5 THEN odTx := #FFFFFFh; ELSE odTx := #003CC8h; END;
        fdTile(odPx, 95, odPx + 35, 131, odCol, #003CC8h);
        TEXTOUT_P(MID("ABCDEFGH", odS, 1), G1, odPx + 4, 98, 2, odTx);
        fdPicto(odPx + 20, 99, odS, odTx);
        IF odSt(odSel) == odS THEN fdRing(odPx, 95, odPx + 35, 131); END;
      END;
    ELSE
      // v3.9: the first line also points to the help (main menu)
      TEXTOUT_P("Tap a tooth, then its stage. Help: main menu.", G1, 8, 97, 1, #000000h, 305);
      TEXTOUT_P("Keys: arrows pick a tooth, Up/Down its stage,", G1, 8, 108, 1, #5A5A5Ah, 305);
      TEXTOUT_P("Enter = stage menu, + = Done, Esc = Cancel.", G1, 8, 119, 1, #5A5A5Ah, 305);
    END;

    // ---------------- the panel: five lines and the age axis ----------------
    RECT_P(G1, 4, 135, 315, 216, #FF4500h, #FFF0DCh);
    // line 1: the selected tooth and its stage label
    IF odSel > 0 THEN
      odL := wtn(odSel) + ": ";                // v3.7b: one space
      IF odSt(odSel) > 0 THEN odL := odL + odStg(odSt(odSel)); ELSE odL := odL + "not staged"; END;
    ELSE
      odL := "No tooth selected.";
    END;
    TEXTOUT_P(odL, G1, 8, 138, 1, #000000h, 305);
    // line 2: Liversidge's figure for the selected tooth, in words
    odL := "";
    IF odSel > 0 THEN
      odS := odSt(odSel);
      IF odS == 0 THEN odL := "Not staged. Willems needs all seven teeth."; END;
      IF odS >= 1 AND odS <= 7 THEN
        odV := fdLvMn(sx, odSel, odS);
        IF odV > 0 THEN
          odL := "Liv. 2006: " + odW + " in " + MID("ABCDEFGH", odS, 1) + ", mean " +
                 fdF2(odV) + " yr, SD " + fdF2(fdLvSD(sx, odSel, odS));
        ELSE
          odL := "Liv. 2006: no figure (too early for the sample)";
        END;
      END;
      IF odS == 8 THEN
        IF sx == 0 THEN
          odL := "Liv. 2006: H at " + fdF2(fdLvH(sx, odSel)) + " yr (girls, the earlier)";
        ELSE
          odL := "Liv. 2006: " + odW + " reach H at " + fdF2(fdLvH(sx, odSel)) + " yr on average";
        END;
      END;
    END;
    IF DIM(odL) > 0 THEN TEXTOUT_P(odL, G1, 8, 149, 1, #003CC8h, 305); END;
    // line 3: Liversidge for the staged teeth below H (fdLvEst)
    IF odLr(1) > 0 THEN
      odL := "Liversidge " + fdF2(odLr(2)) + " yr, SE " + fdF2(odLr(3)) + " indep, " +
             fdF2(odLr(4)) + " together";
    ELSE
      odL := "Liversidge: no tooth below H with a figure";
      IF odLr(9) == 0 THEN odL := "Liversidge: no tooth staged yet"; END;
    END;
    TEXTOUT_P(odL, G1, 8, 160, 1, #003CC8h, 305);
    // line 4: the teeth at H, "at least" the largest Table IX age
    IF odLr(6) > 0 THEN
      odL := "H teeth (" + STRING(odLr(6), 1, 0) + "): at least " + fdF2(odLr(7)) + " yr, Table IX";
      TEXTOUT_P(odL, G1, 8, 171, 1, #003CC8h, 305);
    END;
    // line 5: Willems (fdWEst) -- the sum once all seven are scored
    IF odWr(1) == 1 THEN
      IF odWr(11) == 1 THEN
        // v3.9 (C-F1; second review): at the tables' ceiling (fdWCeil)
        // the sum is not an age
        odL := ("Willems 2001: at the ceiling, " + CHAR(8805) + " ~15.8-16 yr");
      ELSE
        IF sx == 0 THEN
          odL := "Willems 2001: " + fdF2(odWr(2)) + " yr (boys " + fdF2(odWr(5)) +
                 ", girls " + fdF2(odWr(6)) + ")";
        ELSE
          odL := "Willems 2001: " + fdF2(odWr(2)) + " yr, " + odW + ", s.d. " + fdF1(odWr(3));
        END;
      END;
    ELSE
      IF odWr(7) < 7 THEN
        odL := "Willems 2001: " + STRING(odWr(7), 1, 0) + " of 7 staged (needs all 7)";
      ELSE
        // all seven staged, but one stage has no score in the table
        odB := "boys'";
        IF odWr(10) == 2 THEN odB := "girls'"; END;
        odL := "Willems 2001: " + odFdi(odWr(8)) + " at " + MID("ABCDEFGH", odSt(odWr(8)), 1) +
               ": no " + odB + " score";
      END;
    END;
    TEXTOUT_P(odL, G1, 8, 182, 1, #C800C8h, 305);
    // age axis 2 .. 20 yr across x 24 .. 300 (276 px for 18 yr)
    LINE_P(G1, 24, 202, 300, 202, #000000h);
    FOR odI FROM 1 TO 10 DO
      odPx := 24 + IP((2*odI - 2) * 276 / 18);
      LINE_P(G1, odPx, 202, odPx, 205, #000000h);
      odL := STRING(2*odI, 2, 0);
      TEXTOUT_P(odL, G1, odPx - 3*SIZE(odL), 205, 1, #5A5A5Ah);
    END;
    fdDotV(24 + IP(16 * 276 / 18), 191, 201, #FF0000h);     // 18 years
    // each staged tooth below H with a figure: a pale blue tick at
    // its in-stage mean
    FOR odI FROM 1 TO 7 DO
      odS := odSt(odI);
      IF odS >= 1 AND odS <= 7 THEN
        odV := fdLvMn(sx, odI, odS);
        IF odV > 0 THEN
          odPx := 24 + IP((MAX(2, MIN(20, odV)) - 2) * 276 / 18);
          LINE_P(G1, odPx, 198, odPx, 201, #AACDFFh);
        END;
      END;
    END;
    // Liversidge: the estimate as a blue square, and a blue bar of
    // +/- the larger SE (the teeth move together)
    IF odLr(1) > 0 THEN
      odX := 24 + IP((MAX(2, MIN(20, odLr(2) - odLr(4))) - 2) * 276 / 18);
      odY := 24 + IP((MAX(2, MIN(20, odLr(2) + odLr(4))) - 2) * 276 / 18);
      LINE_P(G1, odX, 197, odY, 197, #003CC8h);
      LINE_P(G1, odX, 195, odX, 199, #003CC8h);
      LINE_P(G1, odY, 195, odY, 199, #003CC8h);
      odPx := 24 + IP((MAX(2, MIN(20, odLr(2))) - 2) * 276 / 18);
      RECT_P(G1, odPx - 2, 195, odPx + 2, 199, #003CC8h, #003CC8h);
    END;
    // the teeth at H: "at least", a blue arrow pointing older
    IF odLr(6) > 0 THEN
      odPx := 24 + IP((MAX(2, MIN(20, odLr(7))) - 2) * 276 / 18);
      LINE_P(G1, odPx, 192, odPx, 201, #003CC8h);
      LINE_P(G1, odPx, 194, odPx + 7, 194, #003CC8h);
      LINE_P(G1, odPx + 7, 194, odPx + 4, 192, #003CC8h);
      LINE_P(G1, odPx + 7, 194, odPx + 4, 196, #003CC8h);
    END;
    // Willems: the sum as a magenta mark, a post two pixels wide with
    // a flat cap
    // (v3.8a: from y 193, two pixels below v3.8, clear of the panel text)
    IF odWr(1) == 1 THEN
      odPx := 24 + IP((MAX(2, MIN(20, odWr(2))) - 2) * 276 / 18);
      LINE_P(G1, odPx, 193, odPx, 201, #C800C8h);
      LINE_P(G1, odPx + 1, 193, odPx + 1, 201, #C800C8h);
      RECT_P(G1, odPx - 2, 193, odPx + 3, 194, #C800C8h, #C800C8h);
      // v3.9: at the ceiling (fdWCeil) the sum is a floor, not
      // an age, so the mark gets an arrow pointing older, like the
      // blue H floor's
      IF odWr(11) == 1 THEN
        LINE_P(G1, odPx + 1, 197, odPx + 8, 197, #C800C8h);
        LINE_P(G1, odPx + 8, 197, odPx + 5, 195, #C800C8h);
        LINE_P(G1, odPx + 8, 197, odPx + 5, 199, #C800C8h);
      END;
    END;

    BLIT_P(G0, 0, 0, G1);
    // the soft keys, painted on the screen after the blit
    DRAWMENU({"Clear", "", "", "", "Cancel", "Done"});

    // ---------------- wait for a key or a tap ----------------
    REPEAT
      odK := GETKEY;
      odM := MOUSE;
      odM1 := odM(1);
    UNTIL odK > -1 OR SIZE(odM1) > 0;
    // odAct: 1 Clear, 5 Cancel, 6 Done; 0 nothing
    odAct := 0;
    IF SIZE(odM1) > 0 THEN
      odX := odM1(1); odY := odM1(2);
      fdDrain();                                 // wait for the finger to lift
      IF odY >= 218 THEN
        // the soft-key strip: six 53-px slots, left to right
        odAct := MIN(6, IP(odX / 53.4) + 1);
        IF odAct >= 2 AND odAct <= 4 THEN odAct := 0; END;   // blank keys
      ELSE
        // a tooth?
        FOR odI FROM 1 TO 7 DO
          IF odX >= odX1(odI) AND odX <= odX2(odI) AND odY >= odY1(odI) AND odY <= odY2(odI) THEN
            odSel := odI;
          END;
        END;
        // a stage button (only while they are on screen)
        IF odSel > 0 AND odY >= 95 AND odY <= 131 AND odX >= 6 THEN
          odS := IP((odX - 6) / 39) + 1;
          IF odS >= 1 AND odS <= 8 AND odX <= 6 + (odS - 1)*39 + 35 THEN
            // the same stage again un-stages the tooth
            IF odSt(odSel) == odS THEN odSt(odSel) := 0; ELSE odSt(odSel) := odS; END;
          END;
        END;
      END;
    ELSE
      // the keys remain as shortcuts for everything a finger does
      IF odK == 7 THEN odSel := MAX(1, odSel - 1); END;          // Left
      IF odK == 8 THEN odSel := MIN(7, odSel + 1); END;          // Right
      IF odK == 2 OR odK == 12 THEN                              // Up / Down
        IF odSel == 0 THEN odSel := 1; END;
        IF odK == 2 THEN odSt(odSel) := MIN(8, odSt(odSel) + 1); END;
        IF odK == 12 THEN odSt(odSel) := MAX(0, odSt(odSel) - 1); END;
      END;
      IF odK == 30 THEN                                          // Enter
        // the CHOOSE route, for keyboard users: the stage list on one
        // screen (simple syntax holds up to 14 items); v3.7b: the
        // labels of odStg, Demirjian's wording
        IF odSel == 0 THEN odSel := 1; END;
        odCh := 0;                    // CANCEL must be distinguishable
        CHOOSE(odCh, wtn(odSel),
          "Not staged (skip this tooth)",
          "A  cusp tips, not joined",
          "B  cusp tips joined",
          "C  occlusal enamel complete",
          "D  crown complete to CEJ",
          "E  root shorter than crown",
          ("F  root " + CHAR(8805) + " crown, apex wide"),
          "G  canal walls parallel",
          "H  apex closed"
        );
        IF odCh > 0 THEN odSt(odSel) := odCh - 1; END;
        REPEAT odK := GETKEY; UNTIL odK == -1;
        fdDrain();
      END;
      IF odK == 50 THEN odAct := 6; END;                         // + = Done
      IF odK == 4 THEN odAct := 5; END;                          // Esc = Cancel
    END;
    IF odAct == 1 THEN odSt := {0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0}; END;
    IF odAct == 5 THEN odGo := 0; odRet := {-1}; END;
    IF odAct == 6 THEN odGo := 0; odRet := odSt; END;
  END;
  RETURN odRet;
END;

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// The threshold table (the numbers page).  v3.5 built it inline in
// FDAES() (Threshold Table item); v3.6 moves the same lines here so
// the posterior screen's Table soft key can reach it from both the
// Bayesian Combination and the Threshold Table items.
// ------------------------------------------------------------
fdThrTable(pmu, psd)
BEGIN
  LOCAL tlist, tmsg, tii, tv, pa, pb;
  tlist := {10, 12, 14, 16, 18, 21, 25};
  // v3.9 (C-F9): "label = value", spaced, as everywhere else
  tmsg  := {
    (CHAR(956) + " = ") + fdF1(pmu) + "  sd = " + fdF2(psd),
    "",
    ("Age  P(" + CHAR(8805) + "age)  P(<age)"),
    "---  --------  -------"};
  FOR tii FROM 1 TO 7 DO
    tv := tlist(tii);
    // v3.8a: P(>= age) as the LOWER tail NORMALD_CDF(mu, sd, 2mu - age),
    // which keeps a tiny tail that 1 - NORMALD_CDF would round to 0 in
    // the Prime's 12 digits; P(< age) is a CDF of its own, not 100 - P
    pa := NORMALD_CDF(pmu, psd, 2*pmu - tv) * 100;
    pb := NORMALD_CDF(pmu, psd, tv) * 100;
    tmsg := CONCAT(tmsg,
      {STRING(tv,1,0) + "    " + fdPctN(pa) + "%    " + fdPctN(pb) + "%"});
  END;
  fdPage("THRESHOLD TABLE", tmsg);
END;

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// THE POSTERIOR CURVE (v3.6).  N(pmu, psd) in blue on an age axis,
// the 68 % and 95 % bands as fills under it (the same intervals the
// result page prints: mean +/- 1 SD, and mean +/- 2 SD clipped to
// 0 .. 110), dotted lines at 16, 18 and 21 years with their tail
// probabilities in the panel, and a red threshold line:
//   drag it with a finger -- "P(age >= t)" follows live, from
//     NORMALD_CDF (one call per frame; the curve is cached);
//     on lift it snaps to the nearest 0.5 yr;
//   Left / Right move it 0.5 yr.
//   Soft keys: Table (the numbers page), Forest (the forest plot),
//     Age (type a threshold), Done.  Enter = the same as a menu,
//     Esc = Done.
// Returns {action, threshold}: action 0 done, 1 table, 2 forest.
// The threshold comes back so the caller can reopen at the same t.
// ------------------------------------------------------------
fdPost(pmu, psd, thr0)
BEGIN
  LOCAL poL, poR, poT, poB, poW, poH;
  LOCAL poXlo, poXhi, poYmx, poRow, poJ, poX;
  LOCAL poY, po68a, po68b, po95a, po95b, poThr;
  LOCAL poGo, poRet, poK, poM, poM1, poDn;
  LOCAL poRd, poAct, poP, poI, poIn, poOk;
  LOCAL poCh, poPx, poFit, poL2, poStp, poV;
  LOCAL poTk, poCol;

  poL := 30; poR := 312; poT := 30; poB := 148;
  poW := poR - poL; poH := poB - poT;
  // the result page's intervals (FDAES(), Bayesian Combination)
  po68a := MAX(0, pmu - psd);   po68b := pmu + psd;
  po95a := MAX(0, pmu - 2*psd); po95b := MIN(110, pmu + 2*psd);
  poTk := {16, 18, 21};
  poThr := thr0;
  poGo := 1; poRet := {0, thr0}; poDn := 0; poRd := 1; poFit := 1;
  REPEAT poK := GETKEY; UNTIL poK == -1;
  fdDrain();

  WHILE poGo DO
    // ---- the window: the curve +/- 4 SD, always including 14 .. 23 so
    // the 16, 18 and 21 lines are on it, and widened for a typed age.
    // It is fitted on entry and stays put while the line moves.
    IF poFit THEN
      poXlo := MAX(0, MIN(pmu - 4*psd, 14));
      poXhi := MAX(pmu + 4*psd, 23);
      IF poThr < poXlo THEN poXlo := MAX(0, poThr - 1); END;
      IF poThr > poXhi THEN poXhi := poThr + 1; END;
      // the density ONCE per pixel column, kept as screen rows
      poYmx := NORMALD(pmu, psd, pmu) * 1.15;
      poRow := MAKELIST(0, K, 0, poW, 1);
      FOR poJ FROM 0 TO poW DO
        poY := IP(poB - NORMALD(pmu, psd, poXlo + poJ/poW*(poXhi - poXlo))/poYmx*poH);
        IF poY < poT THEN poY := poT; END;
        poRow(poJ + 1) := poY;
      END;
      // age ticks: a step of 1, 2, 5, 10 or 20 years, about six ticks
      poStp := 1;
      IF (poXhi - poXlo)/6 > 1 THEN poStp := 2; END;
      IF (poXhi - poXlo)/6 > 2 THEN poStp := 5; END;
      IF (poXhi - poXlo)/6 > 5 THEN poStp := 10; END;
      IF (poXhi - poXlo)/6 > 10 THEN poStp := 20; END;
      poFit := 0; poRd := 1;
    END;

    // ---- compose the frame in G1 (only when something changed)
    IF poRd THEN
      DIMGROB_P(G1, 320, 240, #FFFFFFh);
      RECT_P(G1, 0, 0, 319, 22, #FF4500h, #FF4500h);
      TEXTOUT_P("POSTERIOR  N(" + STRING(pmu,2,1) + ", " + STRING(psd,2,2) + ")  age in yr",
                G1, 6, 4, 2, #FFFFFFh, 308);
      // grid at the age ticks
      poV := CEILING(poXlo/poStp)*poStp;
      WHILE poV <= poXhi DO
        poPx := poL + IP((poV - poXlo)/(poXhi - poXlo)*poW);
        LINE_P(G1, poPx, poT, poPx, poB - 1, #E1E4EBh);
        poV := poV + poStp;
      END;
      // the bands: one vertical line per pixel column under the curve,
      // the 95 % interval light, the 68 % interval in the house shade
      FOR poJ FROM 0 TO poW DO
        poX := poXlo + poJ/poW*(poXhi - poXlo);
        IF poX >= po95a AND poX <= po95b AND poRow(poJ + 1) < poB - 1 THEN
          poCol := #D7E6FFh;
          IF poX >= po68a AND poX <= po68b THEN poCol := #AACDFFh; END;
          LINE_P(G1, poL + poJ, poB - 1, poL + poJ, poRow(poJ + 1), poCol);
        END;
      END;
      // the curve over the fills (a segment flat on the axis is skipped)
      FOR poJ FROM 1 TO poW DO
        IF poRow(poJ) < poB OR poRow(poJ + 1) < poB THEN
          LINE_P(G1, poL + poJ - 1, poRow(poJ), poL + poJ, poRow(poJ + 1), #003CC8h);
        END;
      END;
      // 16, 18 and 21 years: dotted grey lines, their tags staggered
      // down the plot so three lines 2-3 years apart never collide
      FOR poI FROM 1 TO 3 DO
        poPx := poL + IP((poTk(poI) - poXlo)/(poXhi - poXlo)*poW);
        IF poPx >= poL AND poPx <= poR THEN
          fdDotV(poPx, poT, poB, #5A5A5Ah);
          TEXTOUT_P(STRING(poTk(poI),2,0), G1, poPx + 2, poT + 1 + 10*(poI - 1), 1, #5A5A5Ah);
        END;
      END;
      // the red threshold line, two pixels wide, with a handle at the top
      poPx := poL + IP((poThr - poXlo)/(poXhi - poXlo)*poW);
      poPx := MAX(poL, MIN(poR - 1, poPx));
      LINE_P(G1, poPx, poT, poPx, poB, #FF0000h);
      LINE_P(G1, poPx + 1, poT, poPx + 1, poB, #FF0000h);
      RECT_P(G1, poPx - 2, poT, poPx + 3, poT + 4, #FF0000h, #FF0000h);
      // axes and the age labels, each centred on its tick
      LINE_P(G1, poL, poB, poR, poB, #000000h);
      LINE_P(G1, poL, poB, poL, poT, #000000h);
      poV := CEILING(poXlo/poStp)*poStp;
      WHILE poV <= poXhi DO
        poPx := poL + IP((poV - poXlo)/(poXhi - poXlo)*poW);
        LINE_P(G1, poPx, poB, poPx, poB + 3, #000000h);
        poL2 := STRING(poV,2,0);
        TEXTOUT_P(poL2, G1, MIN(poPx - 3*SIZE(poL2), 318 - 6*SIZE(poL2)), poB + 4, 1, #5A5A5Ah);
        poV := poV + poStp;
      END;
      // the panel: the live probability first, then the fixed thresholds
      // (v3.8a: the lower tail, as on the result page; see fdPctN)
      poP := NORMALD_CDF(pmu, psd, 2*pmu - poThr);
      RECT_P(G1, 4, 162, 315, 216, #FF4500h, #FFF0DCh);
      // v3.8a: fdEq, so a tail beyond 99.9 % reads "P(age < 18.0) > 99.9%",
      // and P(age < t) is NORMALD_CDF(mu, sd, t) itself, not 100 - P
      // (v3.9: the age through fdF1, which can never turn scientific)
      TEXTOUT_P(("P(age " + CHAR(8805) + " ") + fdF1(poThr) + ")" + fdEq(fdPctN(poP*100)) + "%",
                G1, 8, 165, 2, #C800C8h, 305);
      TEXTOUT_P("P(age < " + fdF1(poThr) + ")" + fdEq(fdPctN(NORMALD_CDF(pmu, psd, poThr)*100)) +
                "%    bands: 68% and 95%", G1, 8, 182, 1, #000000h, 305);
      TEXTOUT_P((CHAR(8805) + " 16: ") + fdPctN(NORMALD_CDF(pmu, psd, 2*pmu - 16)*100) +
                ("%   " + CHAR(8805) + " 18: ") + fdPctN(NORMALD_CDF(pmu, psd, 2*pmu - 18)*100) +
                ("%   " + CHAR(8805) + " 21: ") + fdPctN(NORMALD_CDF(pmu, psd, 2*pmu - 21)*100) + "%",
                G1, 8, 193, 1, #5A5A5Ah, 305);
      TEXTOUT_P("drag or " + CHAR(8646) + " = line (0.5 yr), Ent = more, Esc = done",
                G1, 8, 204, 1, #1E90FFh, 305);
      BLIT_P(G0, 0, 0, G1);
      // the soft keys, painted on the screen after the blit
      DRAWMENU({"Table", "Forest", "Age", "", "", "Done"});
      poRd := 0;
    END;

    // ---- wait for a key or a touch; while a finger is down the loop
    // comes straight back, so the lift is seen at once
    REPEAT
      poK := GETKEY;
      poM := MOUSE;
      poM1 := poM(1);
    UNTIL poK > -1 OR SIZE(poM1) > 0 OR poDn == 1;
    poAct := 0;
    IF SIZE(poM1) > 0 THEN
      poK := -1;                                 // a touch is not a keypress
      IF poDn == 0 AND poM1(2) >= 218 THEN
        // the soft-key strip: six 53-px slots, left to right
        fdDrain();
        poAct := MIN(6, IP(poM1(1) / 53.4) + 1);
        IF poAct == 4 OR poAct == 5 THEN poAct := 0; END;   // blank keys
      ELSE
        IF poDn == 1 OR (poM1(1) >= poL AND poM1(1) <= poR AND poM1(2) >= poT AND poM1(2) <= poB) THEN
          // the finger is on the plot: the line follows it, through the
          // SAME box constants the drawing uses
          poDn := 1;
          poPx := MAX(poL, MIN(poR, poM1(1)));
          // to 0.1 yr, the precision the panel prints, so the age shown
          // and the probability beside it always belong together
          poThr := ROUND(poXlo + (poPx - poL)/poW*(poXhi - poXlo), 1);
          poRd := 1;
        END;
      END;
    ELSE
      IF poDn == 1 THEN
        poDn := 0;
        poThr := ROUND(poThr*2, 0)/2;            // the lift: snap to 0.5 yr
        poRd := 1;
      END;
    END;
    // keys: Left / Right step the line 0.5 yr (onto the half-year grid)
    IF poK == 7 THEN poThr := MAX(0, (CEILING(poThr*2 - 0.000001) - 1)/2); poRd := 1; END;
    IF poK == 8 THEN poThr := (FLOOR(poThr*2 + 0.000001) + 1)/2; poRd := 1; END;
    IF poK == 7 OR poK == 8 THEN
      IF poThr < poXlo OR poThr > poXhi THEN poFit := 1; END;
    END;
    IF poK == 4 THEN poAct := 6; END;                       // Esc = Done
    IF poK == 30 THEN                                       // Enter = menu
      poCh := 0;                    // CANCEL must be distinguishable
      CHOOSE(poCh, "Posterior",
        "Threshold table",
        "Forest plot",
        "Type a threshold age",
        "Done"
      );
      REPEAT poK := GETKEY; UNTIL poK == -1;
      fdDrain();
      IF poCh == 1 THEN poAct := 1; END;
      IF poCh == 2 THEN poAct := 2; END;
      IF poCh == 3 THEN poAct := 3; END;
      IF poCh == 4 THEN poAct := 6; END;
      poRd := 1;
    END;
    // ---- the actions
    IF poAct == 3 THEN
      // a typed threshold: v3.5's "Custom threshold" form, same labels
      poIn := poThr;
      poOk := INPUT({{poIn,[0]}}, "Custom threshold",
                    {"Age (yr):"},
                    {"threshold age in years"},
                    {18});
      REPEAT poK := GETKEY; UNTIL poK == -1;
      fdDrain();
      IF poOk THEN
        // v3.9 (fleet review C-F8, C-F9): kept to 0 .. 110 yr (the clip
        // the 95 % band already uses) and rounded to 0.1 yr, the
        // precision the panel prints.  Unclamped, a typed 1000000 widened
        // the window to a million years and the two grid loops drew
        // about 100,000 lines (1E9 never returned); unrounded, a typed
        // 0.05 printed in scientific form, "P(age >= 5.0E-2)".
        poThr := ROUND(MAX(0, MIN(110, poIn)), 1);
        IF poThr < poXlo OR poThr > poXhi THEN poFit := 1; END;
      END;
      poRd := 1;
    END;
    IF poAct == 1 OR poAct == 2 OR poAct == 6 THEN
      poGo := 0;
      IF poAct == 6 THEN poRet := {0, poThr}; ELSE poRet := {poAct, poThr}; END;
    END;
  END;
  RETURN poRet;
END;

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// THE FOREST PLOT (v3.6).  One row per stored estimate: the point
// as a square (its side grows with the method's inverse-variance
// weight) and +/- 2 SD as a bar in the method's colour.  Under them
// the pooled posterior as a magenta diamond spanning +/- 2 SD, a
// dotted magenta line at the pooled mean, and the PRECISION FLOOR:
// dotted grey marks at the pooled mean +/- 2 x the best single SD,
// with a thin grey bar showing how wide the pool would have been
// without the floor (inverse-variance SD x 1.25).  When the grey bar
// is narrower than the floor marks, the floor was applied -- the
// pool was not allowed to claim more precision than its best
// method, because the methods are not independent (Lamendin,
// Prince-Ubelaker, Johanson and Bang & Ramm all read root
// transparency; v3.9).
//   fmu, fsd, flb  the stored means, SEs and labels
// (v3.8: the Willems module stores "Willems 2001" or "Liversidge
// 2006", longer than the old labels.  The label column is 58 px, nine
// characters, as before, and widens only when a stored label needs
// more, so a pool without those two entries draws exactly as in
// v3.7b.  Willems 2001 keeps Willems's green; Liversidge 2006 is
// teal.)
//   pmu, psd       the posterior as FDAES() computed it (floored)
// The floor arithmetic is recomputed here exactly as in FDAES().
// ------------------------------------------------------------
fdForest(fmu, fsd, flb, pmu, psd)
BEGIN
  LOCAL foN, foLx, foRx, foW, foXlo, foXhi;
  LOCAL foI, foY, foA, foB, foC, foTp;
  LOCAL foMin, foRaw, foStp, foV, foPx, foShow;
  LOCAL foH, foYp, foSz, foAx, foL2, foFl;
  LOCAL foT1, foT2, foT3, foMx;

  foN := SIZE(fmu);
  foShow := MIN(foN, 8);                          // rows that fit
  // v3.8: the label column, 58 px (9 characters at 6.2 px) unless a
  // label shown is longer: "Liversidge 2006" (15) needs 93 px
  foMx := 9;
  FOR foI FROM 1 TO foShow DO foMx := MAX(foMx, DIM(flb(foI))); END;
  foLx := 64;
  IF foMx > 9 THEN foLx := 10 + CEILING(6.2*foMx); END;
  foRx := 306; foW := foRx - foLx; foAx := 168;
  // the pool, recomputed the way FDAES() computes it
  foTp := 0;
  FOR foI FROM 1 TO foN DO foTp := foTp + 1/(fsd(foI)^2); END;
  foRaw := (1/foTp)^0.5 * 1.25;                   // before the floor
  foMin := fsd(1);
  FOR foI FROM 2 TO foN DO
    IF fsd(foI) < foMin THEN foMin := fsd(foI); END;
  END;
  foFl := 0;
  IF foN > 1 AND foRaw < foMin THEN foFl := 1; END;   // the floor applied
  // the window: every bar, the diamond and the raw width, plus 5 %
  foXlo := MIN(pmu - 2*psd, pmu - 2*foRaw);
  foXhi := MAX(pmu + 2*psd, pmu + 2*foRaw);
  FOR foI FROM 1 TO foN DO
    foXlo := MIN(foXlo, fmu(foI) - 2*fsd(foI));
    foXhi := MAX(foXhi, fmu(foI) + 2*fsd(foI));
  END;
  foV := (foXhi - foXlo)*0.05;
  foXlo := MAX(0, foXlo - foV); foXhi := foXhi + foV;
  foStp := 1;
  IF (foXhi - foXlo)/6 > 1 THEN foStp := 2; END;
  IF (foXhi - foXlo)/6 > 2 THEN foStp := 5; END;
  IF (foXhi - foXlo)/6 > 5 THEN foStp := 10; END;
  IF (foXhi - foXlo)/6 > 10 THEN foStp := 20; END;

  DIMGROB_P(G1, 320, 240, #FFFFFFh);
  RECT_P(G1, 0, 0, 319, 22, #FF4500h, #FF4500h);
  TEXTOUT_P(("FOREST PLOT  estimate " + CHAR(177) + " 2 SD"), G1, 6, 4, 2, #FFFFFFh, 308);
  // grid at the age ticks
  foV := CEILING(foXlo/foStp)*foStp;
  WHILE foV <= foXhi DO
    foPx := foLx + IP((foV - foXlo)/(foXhi - foXlo)*foW);
    LINE_P(G1, foPx, 28, foPx, foAx - 1, #E1E4EBh);
    foV := foV + foStp;
  END;
  // one row per method
  FOR foI FROM 1 TO foShow DO
    foY := 34 + (foI - 1)*13 + 5;                 // the row's centre line
    foC := #5A5A5Ah;
    IF flb(foI) == "Willems 2001" THEN foC := #008C3Ch; END;
    IF flb(foI) == "Liversidge 2006" THEN foC := #008080h; END;
    IF flb(foI) == "Lamendin" THEN foC := #003CC8h; END;
    IF flb(foI) == "Johanson" THEN foC := #FF4500h; END;
    IF flb(foI) == "Bang-Ramm" THEN foC := #8B4513h; END;
    // v3.9: Prince & Ubelaker's equations (pool label "P-U 2002"), purple
    IF flb(foI) == "P-U 2002" THEN foC := #6A0DADh; END;
    TEXTOUT_P(flb(foI), G1, 4, foY - 5, 1, foC, foLx - 6);
    foA := foLx + IP((MAX(foXlo, fmu(foI) - 2*fsd(foI)) - foXlo)/(foXhi - foXlo)*foW);
    foB := foLx + IP((fmu(foI) + 2*fsd(foI) - foXlo)/(foXhi - foXlo)*foW);
    LINE_P(G1, foA, foY, foB, foY, foC);
    LINE_P(G1, foA, foY + 1, foB, foY + 1, foC);
    LINE_P(G1, foA, foY - 3, foA, foY + 4, foC);
    LINE_P(G1, foB, foY - 3, foB, foY + 4, foC);
    // the square: half-side 1 to 5 px (side 3 to 11) by the method's
    // inverse-variance weight, (1/SE^2) / SUM(1/SE^2)
    foSz := 1 + IP(4*(1/(fsd(foI)^2))/foTp);
    foPx := foLx + IP((fmu(foI) - foXlo)/(foXhi - foXlo)*foW);
    RECT_P(G1, foPx - foSz, foY - foSz, foPx + foSz, foY + foSz, foC, foC);
  END;
  // the pooled row: a magenta diamond, filled column by column
  foYp := 34 + foShow*13 + 12;
  TEXTOUT_P("Pooled", G1, 4, foYp - 5, 1, #C800C8h, foLx - 6);
  foA := foLx + IP((MAX(foXlo, pmu - 2*psd) - foXlo)/(foXhi - foXlo)*foW);
  foB := foLx + IP((pmu + 2*psd - foXlo)/(foXhi - foXlo)*foW);
  foPx := foLx + IP((pmu - foXlo)/(foXhi - foXlo)*foW);
  FOR foI FROM foA TO foB DO
    foH := 0;
    IF foI <= foPx AND foPx > foA THEN foH := IP(6*(foI - foA)/(foPx - foA)); END;
    IF foI > foPx AND foB > foPx THEN foH := IP(6*(foB - foI)/(foB - foPx)); END;
    LINE_P(G1, foI, foYp - foH, foI, foYp + foH, #C800C8h);
  END;
  LINE_P(G1, foA, foYp, foPx, foYp - 6, #C800C8h);
  LINE_P(G1, foPx, foYp - 6, foB, foYp, #C800C8h);
  LINE_P(G1, foB, foYp, foPx, foYp + 6, #C800C8h);
  LINE_P(G1, foPx, foYp + 6, foA, foYp, #C800C8h);
  // the dotted line at the pooled mean, top to axis
  fdDotV(foPx, 28, foAx - 1, #C800C8h);
  // the unfloored width as a thin grey bar under the diamond, and the
  // floor (+/- 2 x the best single SD) as dotted grey marks
  foA := foLx + IP((MAX(foXlo, pmu - 2*foRaw) - foXlo)/(foXhi - foXlo)*foW);
  foB := foLx + IP((pmu + 2*foRaw - foXlo)/(foXhi - foXlo)*foW);
  LINE_P(G1, foA, foYp + 10, foB, foYp + 10, #5A5A5Ah);
  LINE_P(G1, foA, foYp + 8, foA, foYp + 12, #5A5A5Ah);
  LINE_P(G1, foB, foYp + 8, foB, foYp + 12, #5A5A5Ah);
  IF foN > 1 THEN
    foA := foLx + IP((MAX(foXlo, pmu - 2*foMin) - foXlo)/(foXhi - foXlo)*foW);
    foB := foLx + IP((pmu + 2*foMin - foXlo)/(foXhi - foXlo)*foW);
    fdDotV(foA, foYp - 10, foYp + 13, #5A5A5Ah);
    fdDotV(foB, foYp - 10, foYp + 13, #5A5A5Ah);
  END;
  // axis and age labels
  LINE_P(G1, foLx, foAx, foRx, foAx, #000000h);
  foV := CEILING(foXlo/foStp)*foStp;
  WHILE foV <= foXhi DO
    foPx := foLx + IP((foV - foXlo)/(foXhi - foXlo)*foW);
    LINE_P(G1, foPx, foAx, foPx, foAx + 3, #000000h);
    foL2 := STRING(foV,2,0);
    TEXTOUT_P(foL2, G1, MIN(foPx - 3*SIZE(foL2), 318 - 6*SIZE(foL2)), foAx + 4, 1, #5A5A5Ah);
    foV := foV + foStp;
  END;
  // the panel: why the floor exists, in numbers
  foT1 := "Pooled " + STRING(pmu,2,1) + " yr, SD " + STRING(psd,2,2) + " yr";
  IF foFl THEN foT1 := foT1 + " (floored)"; END;
  IF foN > 1 THEN
    IF foFl THEN
      foT2 := ("inv-var SD " + CHAR(215) + "1.25 = ") + fdF2(foRaw) + " < best single SD " + fdF2(foMin);
      // v3.9: four of the adult methods read root transparency
      foT3 := "the methods share root transparency: no gain";
    ELSE
      foT2 := ("inv-var SD " + CHAR(215) + "1.25 = ") + fdF2(foRaw) + (" " + CHAR(8805) + " best single SD ") + fdF2(foMin);
      foT3 := "no floor needed: grey bar = dotted marks or wider";
    END;
  ELSE
    foT2 := "one method: pooled SD = its SD x 1.25";
    foT3 := "the floor needs two or more methods";
  END;
  IF foN > foShow THEN foT3 := STRING(foN - foShow,2,0) + " more method(s) stored, not drawn"; END;
  RECT_P(G1, 4, 184, 315, 216, #FF4500h, #FFF0DCh);
  TEXTOUT_P(foT1, G1, 8, 186, 1, #C800C8h, 305);
  TEXTOUT_P(foT2, G1, 8, 197, 1, #000000h, 305);
  TEXTOUT_P(foT3, G1, 8, 208, 1, #5A5A5Ah, 305);
  TEXTOUT_P("any key or tap = back", G1, 6, 224, 1, #1E90FFh, 308);
  BLIT_P(G0, 0, 0, G1);
  fdWaitAny();
END;

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// M3 BARS (v3.7; v3.6 drew one unsourced ladder here).  For the
// chosen jaw, the five grades D-H as PAIRED bars of P(age >= 18)
// from Mincer 1993 Table 4 as corrected by the erratum: males in
// the house blue, females in magenta, side by side.
//   - The Setup sex is FILLED; the other sex is a pale bar with a
//     coloured outline.  With the sex unknown both are filled.
//   - The chosen grade has a red ring and a red letter (red = "you
//     are here", the house colour).
//   - Behind the bars, the authors' three zones (p 387) as pale
//     bands: D pale red ("unlikely"), E-G pale orange ("coin
//     toss"), H pale green ("confident") -- the colours of the band
//     at the bottom, which answers for the chosen grade.
//   - A red line at 50 %: the coin toss itself.
//   - Under the letters, the two rows of the table in figures, in
//     the bars' colours (a 21-px bar is too narrow for its label).
// Geometry: 0 % at y 146, 100 % at y 50 (96 px).  Each grade has a
// 54-px column from x 37: D 37-90, E 91-144, F 145-198, G 199-252,
// H 253-306.  In a column starting at x0 the male bar is x0+5 to
// x0+25 and the female bar x0+29 to x0+49.
//   mbPm, mbPf  the jaw's five male and five female values, PERCENT
//   mbJn        the jaw's name, "mandible" or "maxilla"
//   mbSx        the Setup sex: 0 unknown, 1 male, 2 female
//   mbCk        the chosen grade, 1..5 (D..H)
//   mbZn        the authors' zone for that grade, 1..3 (fdZone)
// Any key or tap goes on to the result page (fdWaitAny, as in v3.6).
// ------------------------------------------------------------
fdM3Bars(mbPm, mbPf, mbJn, mbSx, mbCk, mbZn)
BEGIN
  LOCAL mbI, mbX, mbY, mbT, mbL, mbS;
  LOCAL mbC, mbFm, mbFf, mbEm, mbEf;
  mbS := {"D", "E", "F", "G", "H"};
  // outlines always in the sex's colour; fills solid for the Setup
  // sex, pale for the other (both solid when the sex is unknown)
  mbEm := #003CC8h; mbEf := #C800C8h;           // blue, magenta
  mbFm := #003CC8h; mbFf := #C800C8h;           // solid fills
  IF mbSx == 1 THEN mbFf := #F8E1F8h; END;      // male: females pale
  IF mbSx == 2 THEN mbFm := #EBF2FFh; END;      // female: males pale

  DIMGROB_P(G1, 320, 240, #FFFFFFh);
  RECT_P(G1, 0, 0, 319, 22, #FF4500h, #FF4500h);
  mbL := "M3  P(age " + CHAR(8805) + " 18): " + mbJn;              // mbJn is a word
  TEXTOUT_P(mbL, G1, 6, 4, 2, #FFFFFFh, 308);
  // the legend: a swatch for each sex, and which one is filled
  RECT_P(G1, 8, 26, 15, 33, mbEm, mbFm);
  TEXTOUT_P("males", G1, 19, 25, 1, #003CC8h);
  RECT_P(G1, 60, 26, 67, 33, mbEf, mbFf);
  TEXTOUT_P("females", G1, 71, 25, 1, #C800C8h);
  mbL := "filled = Setup sex";
  IF mbSx == 0 THEN mbL := "sex unknown: both filled"; END;
  TEXTOUT_P(mbL, G1, 130, 25, 1, #5A5A5Ah, 186);

  // the authors' three zones first, so everything else sits on them
  RECT_P(G1, 37, 36, 90, 146, #FFEBEBh, #FFEBEBh);       // D:   unlikely
  RECT_P(G1, 91, 36, 252, 146, #FFF0DCh, #FFF0DCh);      // E-G: coin toss
  RECT_P(G1, 253, 36, 306, 146, #E8F6EBh, #E8F6EBh);     // H:   confident
  TEXTOUT_P("unlikely", G1, 39, 38, 1, #FF0000h, 51);
  TEXTOUT_P("coin toss (50:50)", G1, 120, 38, 1, #FF4500h, 130);
  TEXTOUT_P("confident", G1, 256, 38, 1, #008C3Ch, 62);

  // grid and labels every 25 % (the 50 % label red, like its line)
  FOR mbI FROM 0 TO 4 DO
    mbY := 146 - IP(mbI*0.25*96);
    LINE_P(G1, 37, mbY, 306, mbY, #E1E4EBh);
    mbL := STRING(mbI*25,2,0) + "%";
    mbC := #5A5A5Ah;
    IF mbI == 2 THEN mbC := #FF0000h; END;
    TEXTOUT_P(mbL, G1, 34 - 6*DIM(mbL), mbY - 5, 1, mbC);
  END;

  // the bars, one column per grade: males left, females right
  FOR mbI FROM 1 TO 5 DO
    mbX := 37 + (mbI - 1)*54;                   // the column's left edge
    mbT := 146 - IP(mbPm(mbI)/100*96);          // top of the male bar
    RECT_P(G1, mbX + 5, mbT, mbX + 25, 145, mbEm, mbFm);
    mbT := 146 - IP(mbPf(mbI)/100*96);          // top of the female bar
    RECT_P(G1, mbX + 29, mbT, mbX + 49, 145, mbEf, mbFf);
    // the grade letter, red for the chosen one
    mbC := #000000h;
    IF mbI == mbCk THEN mbC := #FF0000h; END;
    TEXTOUT_P(mbS(mbI), G1, mbX + 24, 149, 1, mbC);
    // the two values in figures, centred under the column
    mbL := STRING(mbPm(mbI),2,1);
    TEXTOUT_P(mbL, G1, mbX + 27 - 3*DIM(mbL), 160, 1, #003CC8h);
    mbL := STRING(mbPf(mbI),2,1);
    TEXTOUT_P(mbL, G1, mbX + 27 - 3*DIM(mbL), 171, 1, #C800C8h);
  END;
  TEXTOUT_P("males", G1, 2, 160, 1, #003CC8h);
  TEXTOUT_P("females", G1, 2, 171, 1, #C800C8h);

  // the chosen grade: a red ring round its whole column
  mbX := 37 + (mbCk - 1)*54;
  fdRing(mbX + 3, 52, mbX + 51, 144);
  // the red line at 50 % (a coin toss), and the axes
  LINE_P(G1, 37, 146 - IP(0.5*96), 306, 146 - IP(0.5*96), #FF0000h);
  LINE_P(G1, 36, 146, 306, 146, #000000h);
  LINE_P(G1, 36, 50, 36, 146, #000000h);

  // the source, compactly; then the authors' verdict for the grade
  TEXTOUT_P("Mincer 1993 Table 4 (erratum), whites only",
            G1, 8, 184, 1, #5A5A5Ah, 305);
  fdZone(mbZn, 198);
  TEXTOUT_P("any key or tap = details", G1, 6, 224, 1, #1E90FFh, 308);
  BLIT_P(G0, 0, 0, G1);
  fdWaitAny();
END;

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// One row of the M3 table as a line of text, for the self-check
// page (v3.7): the label, then the five values D..H to one decimal,
// two spaces apart -- e.g. "Md M  6.1  29.4  40.5  56.0  90.1".
// Built from the program's own lists, so the page shows exactly the
// numbers the M3 module uses.
// ------------------------------------------------------------
fdM3Row(rwLb, rwV)
BEGIN
  LOCAL rwS, rwI;
  rwS := rwLb;
  FOR rwI FROM 1 TO 5 DO
    rwS := rwS + "  " + STRING(rwV(rwI),2,1);
  END;
  RETURN rwS;
END;

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// A MISPRINT AND ITS ERRATUM (v3.7): the history of one number in
// the M3 table, for students.  Reached from the M3 module's jaw menu
// and from About.  Page 1 tells the story in words; page 2 draws
// the clue a careful reader could have seen.
//   hsXm, hsXf, hsDm, hsDf  the four rows of the CORRECTED Table 4,
//     in percent (maxilla males, maxilla females, mandible males,
//     mandible females): the same lists the M3 module uses.
// The misprint itself, 69.4, appears in this routine ONLY, where it
// is drawn as the red "as printed" point.  It is never used as a
// probability anywhere in the program.
// ------------------------------------------------------------
fdM3Hist(hsXm, hsXf, hsDm, hsDf)
BEGIN
  LOCAL hsI, hsR, hsX, hsY, hsX0, hsY0;
  LOCAL hsPr, hsRow, hsL;

  // ---- page 1: the story (v3.7a: its footer says what a key does)
  // "vol 38": the erratum page prints no volume; 38 is inferred from
  // the paper's own volume, Mincer et al. 1993, J Forensic Sci
  // 38(2):379-390.  v3.9a: Harris et al. 2010 confirmed from Roger's
  // copy of the book (H:\Books and Papers\Forensic Dentistry, Second
  // Edition.pdf): ch 13, Table 13.12, p 289 prints mandible, males,
  // grade E = 69.4, uncorrected (v3.7-v3.9 said "Table 12", the
  // author manuscript's number).  Still 13 lines, one page.
  fdFtX := "any key or tap = next";
  fdPage("A MISPRINT AND ITS ERRATUM", {
    "Mincer et al., J Forensic Sci, March",
    ("1993, Table 4 (p 384): P(age " + CHAR(8805) + " 18)"),
    "by third-molar grade.  It printed",
    "69.4% for males, mandible, grade E.",
    "",
    "The journal's erratum (vol 38,",
    "p 1524) corrected it to 29.4%.",
    "",
    "The 69.4 was reprinted in the 2010",
    "Forensic Dentistry chapter (Harris",
    "et al., Table 13.12, p 289); it lives",
    "on in copies lacking the erratum.",
    "Next: how a reader could spot it."});

  // ---- page 2: the clue, drawn
  // the mandibular males' row as it was PRINTED in March 1993: the
  // corrected row with grade E put back to the misprint
  hsPr := hsDm;
  hsPr(2) := 69.4;
  DIMGROB_P(G1, 320, 240, #FFFFFFh);
  RECT_P(G1, 0, 0, 319, 22, #FF4500h, #FF4500h);
  TEXTOUT_P("THE CLUE: A ROW THAT DIPS", G1, 6, 4, 2, #FFFFFFh, 308);
  // the legend
  RECT_P(G1, 8, 26, 15, 33, #FF0000h, #FF0000h);
  TEXTOUT_P("as printed 1993", G1, 19, 25, 1, #FF0000h);
  RECT_P(G1, 115, 26, 122, 33, #003CC8h, #003CC8h);
  TEXTOUT_P("erratum", G1, 126, 25, 1, #003CC8h);
  RECT_P(G1, 182, 26, 189, 33, #AAB4C8h, #AAB4C8h);
  TEXTOUT_P("the other 3 rows", G1, 193, 25, 1, #5A5A5Ah, 120);
  // grid every 25 %: 0 % at y 140, 100 % at y 44 (96 px); the
  // grades D..H at x 67, 119, 171, 223, 275 (52 px apart)
  FOR hsI FROM 0 TO 4 DO
    hsY := 140 - IP(hsI*0.25*96);
    LINE_P(G1, 40, hsY, 300, hsY, #E1E4EBh);
    hsL := STRING(hsI*25,2,0) + "%";
    TEXTOUT_P(hsL, G1, 37 - 6*DIM(hsL), hsY - 5, 1, #5A5A5Ah);
  END;
  LINE_P(G1, 40, 140, 300, 140, #000000h);
  LINE_P(G1, 40, 44, 40, 140, #000000h);
  // the other three rows, thin and grey: every one rises D -> H
  FOR hsR FROM 1 TO 3 DO
    IF hsR == 1 THEN hsRow := hsXm; END;
    IF hsR == 2 THEN hsRow := hsXf; END;
    IF hsR == 3 THEN hsRow := hsDf; END;
    FOR hsI FROM 2 TO 5 DO
      LINE_P(G1, 67 + (hsI - 2)*52, 140 - IP(hsRow(hsI - 1)/100*96),
             67 + (hsI - 1)*52, 140 - IP(hsRow(hsI)/100*96), #AAB4C8h);
    END;
  END;
  // the printed row in red, then the corrected row in blue over it
  // (two pixels wide): where they agree only the blue shows, so the
  // red is exactly the part the misprint changed
  FOR hsI FROM 2 TO 5 DO
    hsX0 := 67 + (hsI - 2)*52;
    hsX := hsX0 + 52;
    hsY0 := 140 - IP(hsPr(hsI - 1)/100*96);
    hsY := 140 - IP(hsPr(hsI)/100*96);
    LINE_P(G1, hsX0, hsY0, hsX, hsY, #FF0000h);
    hsY0 := 140 - IP(hsDm(hsI - 1)/100*96);
    hsY := 140 - IP(hsDm(hsI)/100*96);
    LINE_P(G1, hsX0, hsY0, hsX, hsY, #003CC8h);
    LINE_P(G1, hsX0, hsY0 + 1, hsX, hsY + 1, #003CC8h);
  END;
  // the corrected points as blue squares, and the grade letters
  FOR hsI FROM 1 TO 5 DO
    hsX := 67 + (hsI - 1)*52;
    hsY := 140 - IP(hsDm(hsI)/100*96);
    RECT_P(G1, hsX - 2, hsY - 2, hsX + 2, hsY + 2, #003CC8h, #003CC8h);
    TEXTOUT_P(MID("DEFGH", hsI, 1), G1, hsX - 3, 143, 1, #000000h);
  END;
  // grade E: the printed point (red, labelled above-left) and the
  // corrected one (labelled below-right, clear of the lines)
  hsX := 67 + 52;
  hsY := 140 - IP(hsPr(2)/100*96);
  RECT_P(G1, hsX - 3, hsY - 3, hsX + 3, hsY + 3, #FF0000h, #FF0000h);
  TEXTOUT_P("printed " + STRING(hsPr(2),2,1), G1, hsX - 36, hsY - 15, 1, #FF0000h);
  hsY := 140 - IP(hsDm(2)/100*96);
  TEXTOUT_P(STRING(hsDm(2),2,1) + " erratum", G1, hsX + 5, hsY + 5, 1, #003CC8h);
  // the panel: the clue in words, then the lesson (help green)
  RECT_P(G1, 4, 158, 315, 216, #FF4500h, #FFF0DCh);
  TEXTOUT_P("Along each row the values rise from D to H.",
            G1, 8, 161, 1, #000000h, 305);
  TEXTOUT_P("As printed, one row went " + STRING(hsPr(1),2,1) + ", " +
            STRING(hsPr(2),2,1) + ", " + STRING(hsPr(3),2,1) + ":",
            G1, 8, 172, 1, #000000h, 305);
  TEXTOUT_P("up, then down.  The erratum's " + STRING(hsDm(2),2,1) + " fits.",
            G1, 8, 183, 1, #000000h, 305);
  TEXTOUT_P("Lesson: look for errata, and check that a",
            G1, 8, 194, 1, #008000h, 305);
  TEXTOUT_P("table's pattern makes sense before using it.",
            G1, 8, 205, 1, #008000h, 305);
  TEXTOUT_P("any key or tap = back", G1, 6, 224, 1, #1E90FFh, 308);
  BLIT_P(G0, 0, 0, G1);
  fdWaitAny();
END;

EXPORT FDAES()
BEGIN

// ============================================================
// ALL LOCALS - must appear before any executable statement
// (six names to a line, the house rule; same names and order as v3.4)
// ============================================================

// Session state
LOCAL NL, means, sds, lbls, n_est, sex;
LOCAL anc, pmu, psd, pok;

// Loop control
LOCAL ch, ch2, ch_yn, run;

// General computation
//   iok   INPUT return: 1 = OK, 0 = user cancelled
//   smin  smallest contributing SE, used as a precision floor
//   mlt   (v3.9) " x " with the multiplication sign, CHAR(215), for
//         the worked lines of the result pages ("T = 2.5 x 8.98")
LOCAL ae, sv, iok, smin, mlt;

// Setup
LOCAL sx, ax, sxn, axn;

// Willems and Liversidge (v3.8; v3.7b's wm, wf, wsum, wsi, wta, wmn,
// wbse and wse -- the unsourced age tables and the invented SE --
// are gone; the tables live in fdTabs)
//   wtn   the seven tooth names;  wst  the odontogram's seven stage
//         columns (0 = not staged, 1..8 = A..H), or {-1} on Cancel
//   wn    how many teeth are staged;  wti  a loop counter
//   wr    Willems's result (fdWEst's list);  wlr  Liversidge's
//         (fdLvEst's list)
//   wgo   the pool question repeats while 1;  wpk  its answer;
//   wac   what it asked for: 0 nothing, 1 Willems, 2 Liversidge,
//         3 the teaching page
//   wgb, wgg, whb, whg  the self-check's Willems sums (all at G and
//         all at H, boys and girls)
//   wofr  (v3.9) 1 when the Willems age may be offered to the pool:
//         an age (wr(1) = 1) that is not the tables' ceiling (all
//         seven at H, wr(11) = 1; Roger's D7)
LOCAL wtn, wst, wn, wti, wr, wlr;
LOCAL wgo, wpk, wac, wgb, wgg, whb;
LOCAL whg, wofr;

// M3 (v3.7: Mincer 1993 Table 4 as corrected, by sex and by jaw)
//   m3xm, m3xf, m3dm, m3df  the table's four rows, in percent
//     (maxilla males, maxilla females, mandible males, mandible
//     females), set once at INITIALISE
//   m3j   the jaw menu's answer;  m3jn the jaw's name
//   m3pm, m3pf  the chosen jaw's male and female rows
//   m3c   the grade menu's answer;  m3st the grade letters
//   m3z   the authors' zone, 1..3 (fdZone)
//   m3p   P(age >= 18) for the Setup sex, in percent
//   m3sx  the Setup sex as a word;  m3L the result page's lines
// (v3.6's m3pr, the unsourced ladder, and m3i, its verdict text,
// are gone)
LOCAL m3c, m3st, m3p, m3j, m3jn, m3z;
LOCAL m3xm, m3xf, m3dm, m3df, m3pm, m3pf;
LOCAL m3sx, m3L;

// Lamendin 1992 / Prince-Ubelaker 2002 (v3.9: rebuilt; v3.8a's lic,
// lse, lan, licv and lsev -- the unsourced ancestry intercepts and
// SDs -- are gone)
//   lRH, lPh, lTh  root height, periodontosis and translucency
//         heights, mm (the INPUT form)
//   lP, lT  P = lPh x 100 / lRH and T = lTh x 100 / lRH
//   laL, lsL  Lamendin's age and spread (fdLamSD, 12.5)
//   lgp   the P-U group (fdPUg), 0 when P-U has no equation
//   laP, lsP  P-U's age and spread (the Table's RMSE)
//   lok   1 when P-U's age is usable: inside their sample, 25 to 99
//   lpe   P-U's estimate as printed ("< 0" for a negative one)
//   lpk   the pool answer;  lpl  the result page's lines
//   lbad  a refusal message ("" when the inputs are good)
//   llb   the three lines that say what Lamendin's spread is
//   lk    where P-U's coefficients start in fdPUc; then the pool
//         answer as 1 P-U, 2 Lamendin, 0 neither
LOCAL lP, lT, lk, llb, lRH, lPh;
LOCAL lTh, laL, lsL, laP, lsP, lgp;
LOCAL lok, lpk, lpl, lbad, lpe;

// Johanson
//   jlb   (v3.9) the two lines that say what the spread is
LOCAL jA, jS, jP, jC, jR, jT;
LOCAL jval, jsc, jvi, jnc, jse, jlb;

// Bang & Ramm 1970 (v3.9: rebuilt on B&R's own tables; v3.8a's bric,
// brsl, brse, brnm, bricv, brslv, brsev and brT -- the unsourced
// T% line -- are gone)
//   brc   the root: 1 intact (IT), 2 longitudinal section (SS)
//   brt   the tooth, 1..12 (brNm);  brs  the side or root, 1..3
//   brRw  the table row, 1..24;  brX  TM, the mean transparent
//         length, mm (B&R p 4)
//   brV   fdBRAge's {A, SA, B0, B1, B2, table}
//   brNm  the twelve tooth names;  brKd  what the second menu asks:
//         0 nothing (one row), 1 right / left, 2 the molar's root
//   brR1, brR2, brR3  the row for the 1st, 2nd and 3rd answer
//   brAg, brSs  this person's root ages and SAs;  brLn  their lines
//   brgo  another root while 1;  brOk  1 when a root was measured
//   brWh  the root's name;  brMore  "another root?" answer
//   brCb  fdBRCmb's {A, S_A, sum Sa^2};  brL  a page's lines
//   brQ   the other equation's age (near 9.0 mm);  brOt  its table
//   brRs  (v3.9, second review) the table rows already used for this
//         person;  brI  a loop counter;  brDp  1 when a root repeats
//   brFl  1 when a molar root or an upper first premolar is among the
//         person's roots (B&R p 19's advice on the one-person page)
LOCAL brc, brt, brs, brRw, brX, brV;
LOCAL brNm, brKd, brR1, brR2, brR3, brAg;
LOCAL brSs, brLn, brgo, brOk, brWh, brMore;
LOCAL brCb, brL, brQ, brOt, brRs, brI;
LOCAL brFl, brDp;

// Bayesian (wmsg is now a LIST of lines, not a string)
LOCAL idx, prc, tp, ws, c68lo, c68hi;
LOCAL c95lo, c95hi, wmsg, widx, wprc, ww;
LOCAL p16, p18, p21;
//   rlam  (v3.9) 1 when a Lamendin entry is in the pool (the report
//         then says what its 12.5 is)
//   pfl   (v3.9) 1 when the SD floor was applied
LOCAL rlam, pfl;

// Verdict band and posterior screen (v3.6)
//   vpr   P(age >= 18) for the verdict band of a result page
//   pthr  the threshold age on the posterior screen
//   pact, pres  what fdPost asked for next, and its {action, age}
// (the threshold table's LOCALs moved into fdThrTable with its
// lines; the custom-threshold form is the posterior screen's Age key)
LOCAL vpr, pthr, pact, pres;

// ============================================================
// INITIALISE
// ============================================================

NL    := CHAR(10);
fdFtX := "";                    // v3.7a: no one-off footer pending
means := {};
sds   := {};
lbls  := {};
n_est := 0;
sex   := 0;
anc   := 0;
pmu   := 0;
psd   := 0;
pok   := 0;
run   := 1;
ch    := 0;
ch2   := 0;
ch_yn := 0;
ae    := 0;
sv    := 0;
iok   := 0;
smin  := 0;
mlt   := " " + CHAR(215) + " ";  // v3.9: " x ", the multiplication sign

// THE M3 TABLE (v3.7).  Mincer, Harris & Berryman (1993) J Forensic
// Sci 38(2):379-390, Table 4 (p 384), AS CORRECTED by the journal's
// erratum (J Forensic Sci 1993, p 1524).  Empirical probability,
// in PERCENT, of being at least 18 years old, by the grade of
// third-molar formation D, E, F, G, H.  Whites only (US and Canada).
// The H values presume other evidence shows the person is under 25.
// One list per row, in the paper's order.  Used by the M3 module,
// the self-check page and the misprint page (fdM3Hist).
m3xm := {15.9, 27.8, 44.0, 46.8, 85.3};   // maxilla,  males
m3xf := {9.7, 28.4, 50.4, 63.3, 89.6};    // maxilla,  females
m3dm := {6.1, 29.4, 40.5, 56.0, 90.1};    // mandible, males (E: the erratum's value)
m3df := {11.3, 27.4, 43.2, 69.8, 92.2};   // mandible, females

// v3.8: the Willems and Liversidge tables (file-level, see fdTabs)
fdTabs();
// v3.9: Prince & Ubelaker's equations and Bang & Ramm's tables
fdPUTab();
fdBRTab();

// v3.9: the opening page, once per run, before the main menu (Roger,
// 19 Oct 2026, 12:48): what FDAES is and is not
fdFtX := "any key or tap = main menu";
fdPage("FDAES v3.9d", {
  "Forensic Dental Age Estimation",
  "System (Roger Metcalf, 2026)",
  "",
  "TEACHING & DEMONSTRATION AID",
  "Not validated forensic or casework",
  "software. Do not rely on it for",
  "legal purposes. Check every number",
  "against the original papers.",
  "",
  "Start with Setup (sex, ancestry).",
  "Instructions and assumptions:",
  "'How to use / assumptions' on the",
  "main menu."});

// ============================================================
// MAIN MENU - two screens for readability
// (CHOOSE simple syntax actually allows up to 14 options)
// The Prime numbers the items itself (1-9, then A, B, C), so the
// strings carry no numbers of their own.
// ============================================================
WHILE run DO

  // Screen 1 of 2 (v3.9: "How to use / assumptions" is item 5, so
  // "More options..." is 6 and "Exit" 7; Help's internal code is 14)
  ch := 0;                      // CANCEL must be distinguishable
  CHOOSE(ch,
    "FDAES  (1 of 2)",
    "Setup: sex & ancestry",
    "Willems 2001  subadult",
    ("M3 Threshold  P(age " + CHAR(8805) + " 18)"),
    "Lamendin / Prince-Ubelaker",
    "How to use / assumptions",
    "More options...",
    "Exit"
  );

  // Exit (or Cancel): ch := 0 as well, so that no module below runs
  // (7 is also Johanson's internal code)
  IF ch == 0 OR ch == 7 THEN
    run := 0;
    ch := 0;
  END;
  IF ch == 5 THEN ch := 14; END;

  // Screen 2 of 2
  IF ch == 6 THEN
    ch2 := 0;                   // CANCEL must be distinguishable
    CHOOSE(ch2,
      "FDAES  (2 of 2)",
      "Johanson 1971  histology",
      "Bang & Ramm 1970",
      "Bayesian Combination",
      "Threshold Table",
      "Clear / New Case",
      "Verify Calculations",
      "About & Methods"
    );
    // Map ch2 to internal codes 7-13
    IF ch2 > 0 THEN ch := ch2 + 6; END;
    IF ch2 == 0 THEN ch := 0; END;
  END;

  // ================================
  // 1  SETUP
  // ================================
  IF ch == 1 THEN
    sx := 0;                    // CANCEL must be distinguishable
    CHOOSE(sx,
      "Biological Sex",
      "Unknown",
      "Male",
      "Female"
    );
    IF sx > 0 THEN sex := sx - 1; END;

    // v3.9 (Roger's D2): Prince & Ubelaker's own terms.  Their Terry
    // Collection sample was white and black only, so those two pick an
    // equation (with the sex); "Unknown" and "Other" pick none.  anc:
    // 0 Unknown, 1 White, 2 Black, 3 Other (fdPUg reads it).  v3.8a's
    // "European", "African American", "Asian", "Hispanic" rows and
    // their intercepts are gone (they were in neither paper).
    ax := 0;                    // CANCEL must be distinguishable
    CHOOSE(ax,
      "Ancestry (Prince-Ubelaker)",
      "Unknown",
      "White",
      "Black",
      "Other"
    );
    IF ax > 0 THEN anc := ax - 1; END;

    sxn := {"Unknown","Male","Female"};
    axn := {"Unknown","White","Black","Other"};
    fdPage("Setup saved", {
      "Sex:      " + sxn(sex+1),
      "Ancestry: " + axn(anc+1),
      "",
      "Active estimates: " + STRING(n_est,1,0),
      "",
      "Sex Unknown: Willems mixes boys and",
      "  girls 50:50; Liversidge uses the",
      "  combined columns; M3 shows both",
      "  sexes; no P-U equation.",
      "Ancestry Unknown or Other: no P-U",
      "  equation; Lamendin 1992 alone.",
      "Lamendin, Johanson, Bang & Ramm:",
      "  neither sex nor ancestry."});
  END;

  // ================================
  // 2  WILLEMS 2001 (the published method) and LIVERSIDGE 2006
  // ================================
  IF ch == 2 THEN
    // v3.8.  The odontogram stages the seven left mandibular teeth;
    // two methods then read the same stages:
    //   WILLEMS (2001): the boys' or girls' SCORES of all seven teeth
    //     (Tables 1, 2) SUMMED -- the sum is the dental age (fdWEst);
    //   LIVERSIDGE (2006): each tooth's mean age of children in its
    //     stage (Table X), averaged, with two SEs (fdLvEst).
    // The tables are in fdTabs; the header's PROVENANCE CONFIRMED
    // (v3.8) says where every number came from.
    // Self-check (Verify Calculations): all seven at G give 11.74 yr
    // (boys and girls); a boy with I2 F, C G, P1 E gives Liversidge
    // 9.29 yr, SE 0.65 (independent) or 1.11 (together).
    wtn := {
      "I1 Cent.Incisor (31)",
      "I2 Lat.Incisor (32)",
      "C  Canine (33)",
      "P1 1st Premolar (34)",
      "P2 2nd Premolar (35)",
      "M1 1st Molar (36)",
      "M2 2nd Molar (37)"
    };

    // the odontogram: seven stage columns (0 = not staged, 1..8 =
    // A..H), or {-1} for Cancel (nothing computed, nothing said)
    wst := fdOdonto(wtn, sex);
    IF SIZE(wst) == 7 THEN
      wn := 0;
      FOR wti FROM 1 TO 7 DO
        IF wst(wti) > 0 THEN wn := wn + 1; END;
      END;
      IF wn > 0 THEN
        // the two result pages; each returns its routine's list
        wr := fdWPage(wst, sex);              // Willems:    fdWEst
        wlr := fdLPage(wst, sex);             // Liversidge: fdLvEst
        // The pool question offers only what was computed: Willems
        // when wr(1) == 1, Liversidge when wlr(1) > 0.  One answer
        // at most is stored: both read the same seven teeth, so
        // pooling both would count that evidence twice.  The last
        // item opens the teaching page and asks again.
        // v3.9 (Roger's D7, with fleet review C-F1 and the second
        // review's near-ceiling rule): at the Willems tables' ceiling
        // (fdWCeil: teeth 33-37 all at H) the sum says only "at least
        // about 15.8-16", not an age, so it is NOT offered (wofr = 0).
        // (All seven at H also leaves Liversidge with no estimate, only
        // a floor: then nothing is offered at all.)
        wofr := wr(1);
        IF wr(11) == 1 THEN wofr := 0; END;
        wgo := 1;
        WHILE wgo DO
          wpk := 0;                   // CANCEL must be distinguishable
          wac := 0;
          IF wofr == 1 AND wlr(1) > 0 THEN
            CHOOSE(wpk, "Add to Bayesian pool?",
              "Willems 2001 (sum)",
              "Liversidge 2006 (per tooth)",
              "No",
              "Two ways to read the teeth"
            );
            IF wpk == 1 THEN wac := 1; END;
            IF wpk == 2 THEN wac := 2; END;
            IF wpk == 4 THEN wac := 3; END;
          END;
          IF wofr == 1 AND wlr(1) == 0 THEN
            CHOOSE(wpk, "Add to Bayesian pool?",
              "Willems 2001 (sum)",
              "No",
              "Two ways to read the teeth"
            );
            IF wpk == 1 THEN wac := 1; END;
            IF wpk == 3 THEN wac := 3; END;
          END;
          IF wofr == 0 AND wlr(1) > 0 THEN
            CHOOSE(wpk, "Add to Bayesian pool?",
              "Liversidge 2006 (per tooth)",
              "No",
              "Two ways to read the teeth"
            );
            IF wpk == 1 THEN wac := 2; END;
            IF wpk == 3 THEN wac := 3; END;
          END;
          IF wofr == 0 AND wlr(1) == 0 THEN
            CHOOSE(wpk, "Nothing to add to the pool",
              "Go on",
              "Two ways to read the teeth"
            );
            IF wpk == 2 THEN wac := 3; END;
          END;
          IF wac == 3 THEN fdTwoWays(); ELSE wgo := 0; END;
        END;
        // Willems: the sum (or, sex unknown, the 50:50 mixture's mean)
